# Model Inspection Cleanup Review Inventory

**Branch:** `refactor/model-inspection-cleanup`
**Base:** `a4138a613dd643abe12858eec5d1c3beb09e95e7`
**Phase 1 source evidence:** `401259594fe5bca365bb9bfd2e4619f41bfc574d` / permanent run `31234116989`
**Source list:** `docs/reviews/model-inspection-cleanup-source-files.txt`

## Current progress-polish documentation status

The polished implementation now documents immediate secure-start feedback,
five real runtime/worker operation boundaries, UI-only 550 ms normal-motion
milestone pacing with immediate safety/reduced-motion paths, Precision Orbit,
shared vector glyphs, 24/16 px page/card rhythm, five 48 px default-scale
rows, Balanced Centre terminal geometry, responsive 1/2/3-action layouts, and
the exact 50-fixture catalogue through `MI-050`. Hardware Inspection is not
implemented by this branch. Strict Figma-pixel, real Narrator, and
controlled-OS evidence remain open.

All nine Task 11 documentation files were already registered in the source
list, so this docs-only pass adds no inventory path and does not change the
ledger/source-list cardinality of 625/625. The serialized local ladder at
`27934be2687418d7890b677cfcdabf22f059633d` passed Runtime 189/189, Worker
77/77, Debug 19/19 plus 220/220 plus 322/322, Release 717/717, and N-001 1/1,
all with exact definition/result closure and zero adverse counters. Source
freeze, WER, and process gates remained clean. Release isolation scanned 112
files with zero forbidden hits; its external evidence SHA-256 was
`2B2907FFADA8B5D13D96256FF07C1F366FD7E7EEB0D84FCC8CD6A21D20FEB1B7`.
Raw artifacts remain local and untracked. The exact evidence-doc commit still
requires its post-commit FinalSource gate and is not pre-claimed here.

## Inventory and historical evidence ledger

This ledger records one review disposition for every file in the complete cleanup scope. Historical rows retain their Phase 1 evidence; later task rows record subsequent implementation without rewriting that history. The current source list and ledger contain 638 exact, sorted, unique paths. OpenVINO Task 18 cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 638/638 on 2026-08-22. Hardware-visual-alignment Task 4 registers the approved design and plan; its current cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 627/627 on 2026-08-16. Progress-polish Task 10 registers the permanent completion gate; its cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 625/625 on 2026-08-15 and the cleanup verifier passed 3/3. The approved progress-polish planning cleanup reconciliation was recorded at 609/609; the secure-startup Task 1 cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 612/612, truthful-stage Task 2 cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 614/614, progress-polish Task 4 cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 618/618 with exact packaged GREEN 85/85, and progress-polish Task 5 cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 622/622 with exact packaged GREEN 189/189. Progress-polish Task 9 adds the secure-start fixture and checked-in report-generator path; its cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 624/624 on 2026-08-15 and the cleanup verifier passed 3/3. The local Task 9 non-report fixture contracts passed 179/179, the serialized Debug/x64 fixture campaign passed 220/220, external report generation passed 1/1, normal report contracts passed 11/11, and full Contracts passed 357/357. A `none in Phase 1` change means the file was inspected and deliberately left unchanged at that gate, not skipped. The local hosted-equivalent packaged Release/x64 candidate passed the exact two-category filtered suite 686/686 with zero non-passing results; its ephemeral raw TRX SHA-256 was `2FEDADEF0864218B055B3CF31E2D6D91839A1BE53D74EA778170F9BF137B2C41` and the raw file was not staged. Focused subsets passed the 15 new ordinary classes 286/286 and the real packaged N-001 page journey 1/1. Exact-floor Contracts passed 167/167 before the fixture-contract slice; the Task 2 report contracts passed 11/11, all fixture contracts passed 132/132, catalogue contracts passed 31/31, non-report fixture contracts passed 121/121, and the neutral Release fixture build passed with zero warnings/errors. Task 3 Debug/x64 packaged execution passed the three new fixture-runtime classes 59/59 and those classes plus the existing ViewModel suite passed 83/83; Task 3 cleanup reconciled at 577/577 on 2026-08-11, and Task 4 cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 579/579 on 2026-08-12. Task 5 boundary contracts passed 53/53 and full Contracts passed 352/352; its fresh Release/x64 isolation run scanned 112 package files, proved the ReadyToRun main assembly and `resources.pri`, found zero forbidden fixture/gallery path, token or metadata hits, and published canonical evidence only after owned-root cleanup. Task 5 cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 581/581 on 2026-08-12. Task 6 registers twelve durable Debug/x64 fixture-gallery paths and cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 593/593 on 2026-08-12; focused hardening passed 13/13, the complete gallery passed 72/72, the integrated fixture/page suite passed 201/201, Release onboarding passed 29/29, boundary contracts passed 53/53, and full Contracts passed 352/352. Its Release graph contained 61 Compile items and three project references with zero Debug/fixture closure, while the fresh 513-file AppX scan found zero forbidden fixture/gallery path, ASCII or PRI hits. Fixture-catalogue Task 7 registers three Debug/x64 loaded-tree observation components and one packaged screen-contract suite; Task 7 cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 597/597 on 2026-08-12. The complete packaged screen class passed 37/37, the gallery passed 73/73, and their combined gate passed 110/110. Release Figma/presentation/render/accessibility regression passed 150/150; the Release unit build completed with zero errors and one known NETSDK1198 warning. Full Contracts passed 354/354 after the focused stale-oracle regression demonstrated RED then GREEN 1/1, and the Contract Release build completed with zero warnings/errors. Task 7 Release isolation was deferred to the final Task 10 gate; hosted exact-head release evidence remains pending. Fixture-catalogue Task 8 registers six durable preset/responsive paths and cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 603/603 on 2026-08-13. Its serialized Debug/x64 preset class passed 25/25 after a zero-error build; the combined screen/gallery/preset regression passed 135/135; the corrected Release/x64 layout, motion, accessibility and render regression passed 179/179 after a zero-error build with one known NETSDK1198 warning; full Contracts passed 355/355; every packaged run reported zero crash telemetry and zero leftover test processes. Fixture-catalogue Task 9 registers the real-control interaction and exact-lifetime packaged suites. Their authoritative prerequisite passed 19/19 (Interaction 6 and Lifetime 13), and the complete closed Debug/x64 category passed 220/220 with exact class counts 41/12/73/6/13/7/25/37/6. The Debug build completed with zero errors and 14 known warnings; both runs recorded zero WER delta and zero surviving test processes. Task 10 registers the permanent workflow contract and TestFixtures catalogue entry point, pins the measured Debug campaign, and the current cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 607/607 with verifier 3/3 on 2026-08-13. Its first structural Contracts run passed 356/357 and exposed the now-corrected missing `DoNotParallelize`; the final rerun passed 357/357 (`184106CA8DAC97CC206D6D9C292E2AF83CA812610566F3D92633034B37489D21`). That cleanup removed 36 generated `.task9` directories containing 2,687 files and confirmed zero remain. The final local hosted-equivalent Release/x64 campaign retained the exact filter and 686 minimum while passing 691/691 against all 31 protected classes and their 497-execution map, including 39 navigation executions (`E6C1EF3B00A238ED14BE33AE513A8D7E3AC06382F07AAF62A0404EC73B5F255F`). Focused N-001 passed 1/1 (`19257734B69EFDA98AC84FCAFB5C18E76C907ECB0B25B9800C24FFFA10D20422`). Release isolation passed with a ReadyToRun main assembly, 112 scanned files, zero forbidden path/token/metadata hits, zero relevant processes or isolation children, and zero scoped WER events (`E9851004429FEFAF28F35228D8F8001E9F674ED30864B9742A84CFEA696D09B8`).
